# File ownership

Generated on 28 October 2026 from the rebuilt import history. Each file from the 28 October 2026 project package is attributed to the single Jira item whose described deliverable it most directly implements, and was added in that item's import commit, authored by the item's Jira assignee. `git blame`, GitHub's file history and [`.github/CODEOWNERS`](../.github/CODEOWNERS) show the same owner.

Attribution was decided per file after reading it, against the live Jira descriptions, then checked by an independent reviewer and a cross-area consistency review. Shared foundation (configuration, database, authentication, deployment) belongs to the architecture and repository items. Files that no individual Jira item covers stay with the repository owner in a separate import commit. Files added or changed after the import (deployment and backup fixes, contribution records) keep their real commit authorship and are listed at the end.

**Disclosure.** The repository also contains the August prototype history: 47 commits by `omargad` from 2 to 12 August 2026, before the Jira project began on 14 August. Many files credited to teammates below were already in that prototype, unchanged; the *August prototype* column shows the prototype commit that last touched each one. The repository owner reports that this was the credited Jira assignee's work, committed to the prototype by `omargad` for the group, and asked for authorship to follow that Jira credit.
